# text-processing-rs 0.3.0 runtime dependency license texts

Collected from checksum-verified crates named by the pinned Cargo.lock dependency closure for lazy_static, rustfst and flate2. This conservative closure may include platform-specific code not linked on macOS. Source revision: 27d75c401c225771fc16053dc551a887c2fbfc50. No crate build scripts were run.

found [here](https://github.com/golang/go/blob/b10849fbb97a2244c086991b4623ae9f32c212d0/src/strconv/extfloat.go).
